 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	# Closed schemas for `moriarty-bounded-atomic/1`
     2	
     3	Status: S2 provisional corrected candidate, specified-only and not frozen or implemented.
     4	Fresh independent Fable and GPT-6 audits of these exact bytes remain required.
     5	Every record below is closed: all listed fields are required and no other field
     6	is permitted. Arrays are ordered as stated; JSON `null`, JSON number tokens, and
     7	omitted fields are never substitutes. The literal versions in this document are
     8	part of the accepted bytes.
     9	
    10	## Scalar and canonical JSON codec
    11	
    12	`UInt128Text` is `0|[1-9][0-9]*` in the inclusive range `0..2^128-1`.
    13	`SignedExponentText` is `0|-?[1-9][0-9]*` in `-16..16`; `+1` and `-0` reject.
    14	`Digest` is exactly 64 lowercase hexadecimal characters. `Identifier` is 1..64
    15	ASCII characters matching `[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_]*`, is not a grammar keyword, and
    16	is not `constructor`, `prototype`, or `__proto__`. `TextValue` is a Unicode scalar
    17	sequence of at most 256 UTF-8 bytes. `OpaqueBytes` is lowercase, even-length hex.
    18	`FieldLabel` is an Identifier or exactly `asset` or `amount`.
    19	
    20	`moriarty-canonical-json/1` accepts only closed-schema objects, arrays, strings,
    21	and booleans. It rejects numbers, null, duplicate/unknown keys, lone surrogates,
    22	and a decoded value outside its applicable bounds. Object keys are emitted in
    23	increasing unsigned ASCII-byte order. Arrays retain schema order. Encoding has no
    24	whitespace. A string emits `\"`, `\\`, `\b`, `\t`, `\n`, `\f`, and `\r` for those
    25	seven characters; any other U+0000..U+001F character emits lowercase `\u00xx`;
    26	all other Unicode scalar values emit their shortest UTF-8 bytes without escaping.
    27	No Unicode normalization occurs. A received canonical object is accepted only
    28	when decoding and re-encoding produces byte-for-byte identical input.
    29	
    30	The following shared records are exact:
    31	
    32	```text
    33	Span          = {endByte:UInt128Text, startByte:UInt128Text}
    34	SourceRef     = {generatedTag:GeneratedTag, sourceHash:Digest, spans:[Span]}
    35	GeneratedTag  = "Source" | "AmountUnitRetention" | "PolicyCheck" |
    36	                "SettlementCheck" | "StatusDerivation"
    37	UnitComponent = {exponent:SignedExponentText, unit:Identifier}
    38	UnitVector    = [UnitComponent]
    39	BoundsRef     = {boundsHash:Digest, registryId:"moriarty-bounds/1"}
    40	ProgramRef    = {bounds:BoundsRef, coreVersion:"moriarty-core/1",
    41	                 profile:"moriarty-bounded-atomic/1", programHash:Digest,
    42	                 schemaVersion:"moriarty-program-ref/1", sourceHash:Digest}
    43	```
    44	
    45	`Span` is half-open, `startByte <= endByte <= sourceUtf8Bytes`. In
    46	`moriarty-core/1`, every SourceRef has `generatedTag:"Source"` and exactly one
    47	span. All other GeneratedTag spellings are reserved for MC02 lowering and reject
    48	in this Core version; there are no generated-check Core nodes in this profile.
    49	`UnitVector` omits zero exponents, sorts by the ASCII bytes of `unit`, contains no
    50	duplicate unit, has at most eight members, and uses only declared units. Its
    51	empty value is dimensionless for numeric UInt128. Bool and Text expressions,
    52	including comparisons, references, literals, and Not, also carry exactly `[]` as
    53	nonnumeric metadata. Their explicit `type` distinguishes them from UInt128;
    54	unit-vector classification applies only to numeric expressions.
    55	
    56	## Types and values
    57	
    58	The only type records are:
    59	
    60	```text
    61	UIntType     = {tag:"UInt128"}
    62	TextType     = {tag:"Text"}
    63	BoolType     = {tag:"Bool"}
    64	AmountType   = {tag:"Amount", unit:Identifier}
    65	QuantityType = {tag:"Quantity", unitVector:UnitVector}
    66	StoredType   = UIntType | TextType | AmountType
    67	LocalType    = StoredType | BoolType | QuantityType
    68	BareAmountEffectType = {tag:"AmountFromOperand"}
    69	```
    70	
    71	The only value records are:
    72	
    73	```text
    74	UIntValue     = {tag:"UInt128", value:UInt128Text}
    75	TextWireValue = {tag:"Text", value:TextValue}
    76	BoolValue     = {tag:"Bool", value:boolean}
    77	AmountValue   = {tag:"Amount", unit:Identifier, value:UInt128Text}
    78	QuantityValue = {tag:"Quantity", unitVector:UnitVector, value:UInt128Text}
    79	StoredValue   = UIntValue | TextWireValue | AmountValue
    80	LocalValue    = StoredValue | BoolValue | QuantityValue
    81	NamedStoredValue = {name:Identifier, value:StoredValue}
    82	NamedType        = {name:Identifier, type:StoredType}
    83	```
    84	
    85	Maps are never used for typed values. `NamedStoredValue` and `NamedType` arrays
    86	are in declaration order with unique names. An `AmountValue` always carries its
    87	unit, including in arguments, state, effects, obligations, receipts, and hash
    88	preimages. `BoolValue` and `QuantityValue` may exist only as evaluated expressions
    89	and `let` locals. They cannot appear in constants, state, arguments, observations,
    90	set results, effect operands, obligations, or public result state.
    91	
    92	## Source AST: `moriarty-ast/1`
    93	
    94	SourceIntegerToken is canonical unsigned decimal text `0|[1-9][0-9]*`, without
    95	an integer-range restriction at parse time. Source/AST byte and text bounds
    96	still apply. Stage 6 checks every such token against UInt128 and reports
    97	UINT_RANGE; values that already exceed source or AST encoding bounds reject
    98	at the earlier applicable stage. SourceIntegerToken is not an evaluated value.
    99	
   100	
   101	The root is:
   102	
   103	```text
   104	SourceAST = {declarations:[SourceDeclaration], horizon:SpannedUIntToken,
   105	             lifetime:SpannedUIntToken, name:Identifier,
   106	             profile:"moriarty-bounded-atomic/1",
   107	             schemaVersion:"moriarty-ast/1", sourceHash:Digest,
   108	             sourceUtf8Bytes:UInt128Text, span:Span}
   109	SpannedUIntToken = {span:Span, token:SourceIntegerToken}
   110	```
   111	
   112	Source type syntax is represented by `SourceType = {tag:"UInt128",span:Span} |
   113	{tag:"Text",span:Span} | {tag:"Amount",unit:Identifier,span:Span} |
   114	{tag:"BareAmount",span:Span}`. `BareAmount` is legal only in a source effect
   115	field. Source literals are exactly:
   116	
   117	```text
   118	UIntLiteral   = {span:Span, tag:"UIntLiteral", token:SourceIntegerToken}
   119	TextLiteral   = {decoded:TextValue, span:Span, tag:"TextLiteral", token:string}
   120	AmountLiteral = {span:Span, tag:"AmountLiteral", token:SourceIntegerToken,
   121	                 unit:Identifier}
   122	BoolLiteral   = {span:Span, tag:"BoolLiteral", value:boolean}
   123	```
   124	
   125	`TextLiteral.token` is the exact JSON string token including quotes; `decoded`
   126	is its decoded scalar sequence. `SourceExpression` is exactly one of:
   127	
   128	```text
   129	{literal:SourceLiteral, span:Span, tag:"Literal"}
   130	{span:Span, tag:"Remaining"}
   131	{name:Identifier, span:Span, tag:"StateRef"|"ArgRef"|"ObservationRef"|"ConstRef"|"LocalRef"}
   132	{denominator:SourceExpression, numerator:SourceExpression, span:Span, tag:"FloorDiv"}
   133	{left:SourceExpression, right:SourceExpression, span:Span,
   134	 tag:"Add"|"Sub"|"Mul"|"Eq"|"Lt"|"Lte"|"Gt"|"Gte"|"And"|"Or"}
   135	{operand:SourceExpression, span:Span, tag:"Not"}
   136	```
   137	
   138	Declarations and statements are exactly:
   139	
   140	```text
   141	ReserveDecl     = {action:Identifier, closure:Identifier, span:Span, tag:"ReserveDecl"}
   142	UnitDecl        = {name:Identifier, span:Span, tag:"UnitDecl"}
   143	ConstDecl       = {name:Identifier, span:Span, tag:"ConstDecl", type:SourceType, value:SourceLiteral}
   144	StateDecl       = {name:Identifier, span:Span, tag:"StateDecl", type:SourceType, value:SourceLiteral}
   145	ObservationDecl = {name:Identifier, span:Span, tag:"ObservationDecl", type:SourceType}
   146	SettlementDecl  = {asset:TextLiteral, name:Identifier, quantum:AmountLiteral, span:Span, tag:"SettlementDecl"}
   147	PolicyWriteTarget  = {action:Identifier, field:Identifier, span:Span, tag:"Write"}
   148	PolicyEffectTarget = {action:Identifier, field:FieldLabel, ordinal:SourceIntegerToken, span:Span, tag:"Effect"}
   149	PolicyRoundingNone  = {span:Span, tag:"None"}
   150	PolicyRoundingFloor = {action:Identifier, local:Identifier, span:Span, tag:"Floor"}
   151	FieldPolicyDecl = {comparison:TextLiteral, derivation:TextLiteral, name:Identifier,
   152	                   proof:TextLiteral, remainder:TextLiteral,
   153	                   rounding:PolicyRoundingNone|PolicyRoundingFloor, span:Span,
   154	                   tag:"FieldPolicyDecl", targets:[PolicyWriteTarget|PolicyEffectTarget],
   155	                   unit:Identifier}
   156	EpisodeStatusDecl   = {field:Identifier, literal:SourceLiteral, span:Span, tag:"EpisodeStatusDecl"}
   157	NotionalStatusDecl  = {field:Identifier, span:Span, tag:"NotionalStatusDecl"}
   158	NoNotionalStatusDecl= {span:Span, tag:"NoNotionalStatusDecl"}
   159	EffectFieldDecl = {label:FieldLabel, span:Span, type:SourceType}
   160	EffectDecl      = {fields:[EffectFieldDecl], kind:EffectKind, span:Span, tag:"EffectDecl"}
   161	Parameter       = {name:Identifier, span:Span, type:SourceType}
   162	GuardStatement  = {condition:SourceExpression, message:TextLiteral, span:Span, tag:"Guard"}
   163	LetStatement    = {expression:SourceExpression, name:Identifier, span:Span, tag:"Let"}
   164	SetStatement    = {expression:SourceExpression, field:Identifier, span:Span, tag:"Set"}
   165	EffectFieldExpr = {expression:SourceExpression, label:FieldLabel, span:Span}
   166	EmitStatement   = {fields:[EffectFieldExpr], kind:EffectKind, span:Span, tag:"Emit"}
   167	ActionDecl      = {name:Identifier, parameters:[Parameter], span:Span,
   168	                   statements:[GuardStatement|LetStatement|SetStatement|EmitStatement],
   169	                   tag:"ActionDecl"}
   170	```
   171	
   172	`SourceDeclaration` is the union of UnitDecl, ConstDecl, StateDecl, ObservationDecl,
   173	SettlementDecl, FieldPolicyDecl, the three status variants, EffectDecl, ActionDecl,
   174	and ReserveDecl. `EffectKind` is exactly `Transfer`, `Fee`,
   175	`DueCreated`, or `DueSettled`. Field labels are the grammar's permitted labels.
   176	Declarations and statements preserve source order.
   177	
   178	## Exact source spans and structural lowering
   179	
   180	Offsets count original UTF-8 bytes, with no newline or Unicode normalization.
   181	Every nonempty syntax span begins at the first byte of its first token and ends
   182	immediately after its last token. Interior whitespace is included; leading and
   183	trailing whitespace is excluded. The SourceAST span runs from `agreement` through
   184	the final `}`; sourceUtf8Bytes also counts whitespace outside that span.
   185	
   186	- Declarations and statements include their terminating `;` or closing `}`.
   187	  An action includes its keyword, parameters and complete block. A policy or
   188	  effect declaration ends at its `}`. ReserveDecl covers `reserve` through `;`.
   189	- SourceType spans its type keyword through the last type token, including `>`
   190	  for Amount<Unit>. Parameter spans its name through its type. EffectFieldDecl
   191	  spans its label through `;`. EffectFieldExpr spans its label through the final
   192	  token of its expression, excluding a following comma or closing effect brace.
   193	- SpannedUIntToken covers only its decimal token. UIntLiteral, AmountLiteral and
   194	  TextLiteral produced by `uint(...)`, `amount(...)` and `text(...)` cover the
   195	  entire wrapper through `)`. Their `token` fields retain only the inner decimal
   196	  token or exact quoted JSON token. BoolLiteral covers `true` or `false`.
   197	  A TextLiteral used for a bare JSON token (policy documentary fields or guard
   198	  message) covers only that quoted token; no `text(...)` wrapper is invented.
   199	- A Literal SourceExpression initially has the same span as its child literal.
   200	  References cover every token (`state` through the identifier for StateRef);
   201	  Remaining covers its token. Not spans `not` through its operand. Binary nodes
   202	  span from the left child's start through the right child's end. FloorDiv spans
   203	  `floor_div` through its closing `)`.
   204	- Parentheses introduce no SourceExpression or Core node. Parsing `(expression)`
   205	  replaces only the enclosed expression root's span with the interval from that
   206	  `(` through its matching `)`. Repeat this rule outward for nested parentheses.
   207	  Descendant spans are unchanged, including the literal child of a Literal node.
   208	  Thus `((uint(1)))` has expression span `[0,11)` and UIntLiteral span `[2,9)`.
   209	- Policy targets include their complete write/effect wrapper; rounding Floor
   210	  includes `floor(...)` and rounding None includes only `none`.
   211	
   212	Core lowering preserves tree structure and maps each expression sourceRef to
   213	`{generatedTag:"Source",sourceHash:SourceAST.sourceHash,spans:[expression.span]}`.
   214	Core instructions and actions similarly use their exact source statement/action
   215	span. No extra span normalization, parenthesis node, or generated Core node is
   216	permitted. Literal lowering changes syntax to the corresponding tagged value,
   217	without retaining the separate literal-child span in Core. TypedAnnotation uses
   218	the same expression SourceRef. These rules apply before hashing the manifest.
   219	
   220	## Typed program: `moriarty-typed-program/1`
   221	
   222	```text
   223	TypedProgram = {annotations:[TypedAnnotation], profile:"moriarty-bounded-atomic/1",
   224	                schemaVersion:"moriarty-typed-program/1", source:SourceAST}
   225	TypedAnnotation = {nodeId:Identifier, resolution:Resolution, sourceRef:SourceRef,
   226	                   type:LocalType, unitVector:UnitVector}
   227	Resolution = {tag:"Literal"|"Remaining"} |
   228	             {declaration:Identifier, tag:"State"|"Argument"|"Observation"|"Constant"} |
   229	             {action:Identifier, local:Identifier, tag:"Local"} |
   230	             {tag:"Operator"}
   231	```
   232	
   233	There is exactly one annotation for every expression node, in declaration,
   234	statement, then expression preorder. `nodeId` is `a_<action-index>_s_<statement-index>_e_<preorder-index>`.
   235	All indexes are zero-based canonical decimal without padding. Action indexes
   236	count only ActionDecl nodes in source order. Statement indexes count every
   237	statement in the action, and expression preorder restarts at zero per statement.
   238	Visit each expression root before its children; binary children are left then
   239	right, FloorDiv children numerator then denominator, and Not has one operand.
   240	For Emit, concatenate the preorder walks of each field expression in source field
   241	order into one sequence; do not number field wrappers or literal children.
   242	Parentheses add no node or index. Resolution must name the unique declaration.
   243	
   244	Worked example from loan.moriarty: `accrue` is action 0 and its
   245	`let interest_calculated = floor_div(interest_numerator, interest_denominator);`
   246	is statement 5. The instruction ID is `a_0_s_5`. Its exact expression IDs are:
   247	
   248	| Expression | nodeId |
   249	|---|---|
   250	| FloorDiv root | `a_0_s_5_e_0` |
   251	| LocalRef interest_numerator | `a_0_s_5_e_1` |
   252	| LocalRef interest_denominator | `a_0_s_5_e_2` |
   253	
   254	The policy rounding node for that let is `a_0_s_5_e_0`. Statement 6 restarts
   255	at `a_0_s_6_e_0`; JSON key sorting never changes this traversal order.
   256	
   257	## Core, policies, schemas, and semantic manifest
   258	
   259	Core expressions are exactly these closed variants. `CoreExpression` denotes
   260	their union; Core evaluation follows the recorded tree order, not a reassociated
   261	form.
   262	
   263	```text
   264	{nodeId:Identifier, sourceRef:SourceRef, tag:"Literal", type:LocalType,
   265	 unitVector:UnitVector, value:LocalValue}
   266	{nodeId:Identifier, sourceRef:SourceRef, tag:"Remaining", type:UIntType,
   267	 unitVector:UnitVector}
   268	{declaration:Identifier, nodeId:Identifier, sourceRef:SourceRef,
   269	 tag:"StateRef"|"ArgRef"|"ObservationRef"|"ConstRef", type:StoredType,
   270	 unitVector:UnitVector}
   271	{action:Identifier, local:Identifier, nodeId:Identifier, sourceRef:SourceRef,
   272	 tag:"LocalRef", type:LocalType, unitVector:UnitVector}
   273	{denominator:CoreExpression, nodeId:Identifier, numerator:CoreExpression,
   274	 sourceRef:SourceRef, tag:"FloorDiv", type:LocalType, unitVector:UnitVector}
   275	{left:CoreExpression, nodeId:Identifier, right:CoreExpression,
   276	 sourceRef:SourceRef, tag:"Add"|"Sub"|"Mul"|"Eq"|"Lt"|"Lte"|"Gt"|"Gte"|"And"|"Or",
   277	 type:LocalType, unitVector:UnitVector}
   278	{nodeId:Identifier, operand:CoreExpression, sourceRef:SourceRef, tag:"Not",
   279	 type:BoolType, unitVector:UnitVector}
   280	```
   281	
   282	```text
   283	CoreGuard = {condition:CoreExpression, message:TextValue, sourceRef:SourceRef,
   284	             statementId:Identifier, tag:"Guard"}
   285	CoreLet   = {expression:CoreExpression, name:Identifier, sourceRef:SourceRef,
   286	             statementId:Identifier, tag:"Let", type:LocalType, unitVector:UnitVector}
   287	PolicyUse = {tag:"NonFinancial"} | {name:Identifier, tag:"Financial"}
   288	CoreSet   = {expression:CoreExpression, field:Identifier, policy:PolicyUse,
   289	             sourceRef:SourceRef, statementId:Identifier, tag:"Set"}
   290	CoreEffectPolicy = {field:FieldLabel, policy:Identifier}
   291	CoreEmit  = {fields:[{expression:CoreExpression,label:FieldLabel}], kind:EffectKind,
   292	             ordinal:UInt128Text, policies:[CoreEffectPolicy], sourceRef:SourceRef,
   293	             statementId:Identifier, tag:"Emit"}
   294	CoreInstruction = CoreGuard | CoreLet | CoreSet | CoreEmit
   295	CoreAction = {actorParameter:"actor", arguments:[NamedType], instructions:[CoreInstruction],
   296	              name:Identifier, resourceCounts:ActionResourceCounts, sourceRef:SourceRef}
   297	ActionResourceCounts = {effects:UInt128Text, expressionDepth:UInt128Text,
   298	                        expressionNodes:UInt128Text, instructions:UInt128Text,
   299	                        locals:UInt128Text}
   300	CoreProgram = {actions:[CoreAction], coreVersion:"moriarty-core/1",
   301	               schemaVersion:"moriarty-core-program/1"}
   302	```
   303	
   304	Statement IDs are `a_<action-index>_s_<statement-index>`. Emit ordinal is its
   305	zero-based position among emits in that action. Each action must have exactly one
   306	`actor: Text` parameter; it is the `actorParameter`. This makes the authenticated
   307	principal-to-actor check mandatory rather than a convention inferred from guards.
   308	
   309	Each declared effect kind occurs exactly once and must use its standard schema:
   310	
   311	```text
   312	Transfer   {asset:Text, from:Text, to:Text, amount:AmountFromOperand}
   313	Fee        {asset:Text, from:Text, to:Text, amount:AmountFromOperand}
   314	DueCreated {due_id:Text, debtor:Text, creditor:Text, denomination:Text, amount:AmountFromOperand}
   315	DueSettled {due_id:Text, debtor:Text, creditor:Text, denomination:Text,
   316	            amount:AmountFromOperand, asset:Text}
   317	```
   318	
   319	Field order above is semantic declaration order. Missing, reordered, duplicated,
   320	or additional fields reject. Every emitted kind must be declared. An unused kind
   321	may be omitted. The manifest uses the fixed schema for each declared kind.
   322	
   323	For Amount writes, `PolicyUse` is Financial and names the unique checked policy.
   324	For UInt128 or Text writes it is exactly NonFinancial. A financial target cannot
   325	be assigned to a nonfinancial write. CoreEmit policy entries follow financial
   326	field declaration order and contain exactly one entry per Amount field.
   327	
   328	```text
   329	SettlementBinding = {asset:TextValue, name:Identifier, quantum:AmountValue, unit:Identifier}
   330	WritePolicyTarget  = {action:Identifier, field:Identifier, tag:"Write"}
   331	EffectPolicyTarget = {action:Identifier, field:FieldLabel, ordinal:UInt128Text, tag:"Effect"}
   332	RoundingNode       = {tag:"None"} |
   333	                     {action:Identifier, coreNodeId:Identifier, local:Identifier, tag:"Floor"}
   334	FieldPolicy = {comparisonPolicy:TextValue, derivation:TextValue, name:Identifier,
   335	               proofStatement:TextValue, remainderDisposition:TextValue,
   336	               roundingNode:RoundingNode, targets:[WritePolicyTarget|EffectPolicyTarget],
   337	               unit:Identifier}
   338	EpisodeRule = {field:Identifier, literal:StoredValue, tag:"ClosedWhenEqual"}
   339	AgreementRule = {field:Identifier, tag:"RemainingNotional"} |
   340	                {tag:"NoRemainingNotional"}
   341	StatusRules = {agreement:AgreementRule, episode:EpisodeRule}
   342	ReserveRule = {action:Identifier, closure:Identifier}
   343	ClaimRequirement = {claimId:TextValue,
   344	                    kind:"ContractProperty"|"IntentRefinement"|
   345	                         "TransitionValidity"|"PredecessorHistory"}
   346	```
   347	
   348	Policy documentary strings are opaque text. Their exact bytes are program-hash
   349	bound but are not parsed, compared with expressions, or treated as a proof or
   350	safety predicate. `proofStatement` is an identifier for a mandatory future proof
   351	claim, not evidence that the claim exists.
   352	
   353	The manifest claim requirements are generated deterministically: include
   354	ContractProperty `bounded_profile_safety_v1` and each distinct policy
   355	proofStatement, IntentRefinement `atomic_intent_refinement_v1`,
   356	TransitionValidity `bounded_atomic_transition_v1`, and PredecessorHistory
   357	`bounded_history_compliance_v1`. No caller may add or omit requirements. The
   358	built-ins denote the typed/bounded safety, authority refinement, exact transition,
   359	and predecessor-history predicates defined by this profile. Policy-specific
   360	predicates must be supplied by the profile-bound proof backend; an unknown
   361	policy claim fails closed rather than becoming a vacuous assertion.
   362	
   363	`requiredClaims` is sorted by `(kind ASCII, claimId UTF-8)`, has no duplicate
   364	pair, and contains at least one requirement of each of the four kinds. Contract
   365	property requirements include every policy `proofStatement`; the other three
   366	kinds are the mandatory intent/refinement, transition, and predecessor-history
   367	acceptance predicates. A claim list is specification of proof obligations, not
   368	evidence that any proof system implements them.
   369	
   370	The exact hashable semantic manifest is:
   371	
   372	```text
   373	SemanticManifest = {
   374	  bounds:BoundsRef,
   375	  constants:[NamedStoredValue],
   376	  core:CoreProgram,
   377	  effectSchemas:[{fields:[{label:FieldLabel,type:StoredType|BareAmountEffectType}],kind:EffectKind}],
   378	  horizon:UInt128Text,
   379	  initialState:[NamedStoredValue],
   380	  lifetime:UInt128Text,
   381	  name:Identifier,
   382	  observationSchema:[NamedType],
   383	  policies:[FieldPolicy],
   384	  profile:"moriarty-bounded-atomic/1",
   385	  schemaVersion:"moriarty-semantic-manifest/1",
   386	  settlementBindings:[SettlementBinding],
   387	  sourceHash:Digest,
   388	  stateSchema:[NamedType],
   389	  statusRules:StatusRules,
   390	  requiredClaims:[ClaimRequirement],
   391	  reserveRules:[ReserveRule],
   392	  units:[Identifier]
   393	}
   394	BoundProgram = {manifest:SemanticManifest, programHash:Digest,
   395	                schemaVersion:"moriarty-program/1"}
   396	```
   397	
   398	Arrays follow their source declaration order, including reserveRules and units.
   399	Policy targets follow their source list; requiredClaims alone uses its specified
   400	(kind,claimId) sort. Reserve metadata resolves over the complete action table. There is exactly one
   401	bounds encoding: `BoundsRef`; the numeric `bounds.json` object is never embedded.
   402	The full `BoundProgram`, including Core expressions, is checked against the
   403	program-manifest limits in `bounds.json`. The effective joint bound is the actual
   404	aggregate byte/depth/node/count result; the per-action maxima do not promise that
   405	64 maximum actions fit. The compact `ProgramRef` is the only program object in a
   406	signing statement and never contains `manifest`, `core`, or AST nodes.
   407	
   408	## Hash preimages
   409	
   410	The single complete domain registry is `bounds.json.domainRegistry`; the formulas
   411	below apply those registry entries and do not introduce additional domains.
   412	Concatenation below is byte concatenation and `00` is one zero byte:
   413	
   414	```text
   415	sourceHash  = SHA256(original source UTF-8 bytes)
   416	boundsHash  = SHA256(UTF8("MORIARTY-BOUNDS-bounded-atomic/1") || 00 || exact bounds.json bytes)
   417	programHash = SHA256(UTF8("MORIARTY-PROGRAM-bounded-atomic/1") || 00 || canonical(SemanticManifest))
   418	claimRoot   = SHA256(UTF8("MORIARTY-CLAIMS-bounded-atomic/1") || 00 || canonical(SemanticManifest.requiredClaims))
   419	genesisHash = SHA256(UTF8("MORIARTY-GENESIS-bounded-atomic/1") || 00 || canonical(GenesisBody))
   420	stateHash   = SHA256(UTF8("MORIARTY-STATE-bounded-atomic/1") || 00 || canonical(StateBody))
   421	actionHash  = SHA256(UTF8("MORIARTY-ACTION-bounded-atomic/1") || 00 || canonical(ActionCall))
   422	observationsHash = SHA256(UTF8("MORIARTY-OBSERVATIONS-bounded-atomic/1") || 00 || canonical(ObservationSet))
   423	statementDigest = SHA256(UTF8(authority.domain) || 00 || canonical(authority.statement))
   424	authorityDigest = SHA256(UTF8("MORIARTY-AUTHORITY-bounded-atomic/1") || 00 || canonical(Authority))
   425	proofContextHash = SHA256(UTF8("MORIARTY-PROOF-CONTEXT-bounded-atomic/1") || 00 || canonical(ProofContext))
   426	traceHash   = SHA256(UTF8("MORIARTY-TRACE-bounded-atomic/1") || 00 || canonical(CompleteBody))
   427	proofDigest = SHA256(resolved raw proof bytes)
   428	```
   429	
   430	None of the preimages contains the digest it defines. `BoundProgram`, genesis,
   431	state, and Complete wrappers carry the separately computed digest. A verifier
   432	recomputes it; omission-by-convention is not used.
   433	
   434	## Genesis, state, observations, and action
   435	
   436	```text
   437	ExecutionDomain = {deployment:TextValue, network:TextValue}
   438	PrincipalBinding   = {actor:TextValue, principal:TextValue}
   439	ObservationBinding = {authenticationPolicy:TextValue, name:Identifier,
   440	                      provider:TextValue}
   441	GenesisBody = {bounds:BoundsRef, domain:ExecutionDomain, horizon:UInt128Text, initialState:[NamedStoredValue],
   442	               instanceId:TextValue, lifetime:UInt128Text,
   443	               observationBindings:[ObservationBinding], principalBindings:[PrincipalBinding],
   444	               profile:"moriarty-bounded-atomic/1", program:ProgramRef,
   445	               requiredClaimRoot:Digest, schemaVersion:"moriarty-genesis-body/1"}
   446	Genesis = {body:GenesisBody, genesisHash:Digest, schemaVersion:"moriarty-genesis/1"}
   447	
   448	ObligationRecord = {amount:AmountValue, creditor:TextValue, debtor:TextValue,
   449	                    denomination:TextValue, dueId:TextValue,
   450	                    status:"Outstanding"|"Settled"}
   451	EpisodeStatus = "Open" | "Closed"
   452	AgreementStatus = "Outstanding" | "NoOutstanding"
   453	RemainingNotional = {amount:AmountValue, tag:"Amount"} | {tag:"NotApplicable"}
   454	StateBody = {agreementStatus:AgreementStatus, episodeStatus:EpisodeStatus,
   455	             genesisHash:Digest, instanceId:TextValue, obligations:[ObligationRecord],
   456	             profile:"moriarty-bounded-atomic/1", programHash:Digest,
   457	             remaining:UInt128Text, remainingNotional:RemainingNotional,
   458	             revision:UInt128Text, schemaVersion:"moriarty-state-body/1",
   459	             values:[NamedStoredValue]}
   460	StateEnvelope = {body:StateBody, schemaVersion:"moriarty-state/1", stateHash:Digest}
   461	
   462	ObservationValue = {evidenceDigest:Digest, name:Identifier, provider:TextValue,
   463	                    value:StoredValue}
   464	ObservationSet = {observations:[ObservationValue], schemaVersion:"moriarty-observations/1"}
   465	ActionCall = {arguments:[NamedStoredValue], name:Identifier,
   466	              schemaVersion:"moriarty-action/1"}
   467	```
   468	
   469	ObservationValue.evidenceDigest is an opaque external digest. The profile defines
   470	no preimage or algorithm for it; the genesis authenticationPolicy and trusted
   471	wrapper must validate it against the exact provider/name/value. Signature.algorithm
   472	is likewise an opaque external algorithm identifier, restricted by the deployment's
   473	trusted wrapper; an unsupported algorithm fails signature verification. Neither
   474	field permits an unvalidated client assertion.
   475	
   476	Principal bindings are unique by both principal and actor. Observation bindings
   477	are unique by name and cover the manifest schema exactly; `now:UInt128` is
   478	mandatory. The wrapper authenticates the principal, provider, evidence, claim
   479	root, and genesis before evaluation. These schemas bind those external checks;
   480	they do not implement cryptography, oracle truth, ledger ordering, or custody.
   481	
   482	## Authority and compact signing objects
   483	
   484	The new profile does not alter or reinterpret historical domains
   485	`MORIARTY-SIGN-v1` and `MORIARTY-OUTCOME-SIGN-v1`. Historical statements are not
   486	valid instances of these new schemas.
   487	
   488	```text
   489	Validity = {notBefore:UInt128Text, notAfterExclusive:UInt128Text}
   490	Signature = {algorithm:TextValue, bytes:OpaqueBytes, keyId:TextValue}
   491	ExactEffect = {effect:EffectRecord}
   492	ExactWrite  = {field:Identifier, value:StoredValue}
   493	DebitCap    = {actor:TextValue, asset:TextValue, maximumLedgerAmount:UInt128Text}
   494	NetGoal     = {actor:TextValue, asset:TextValue, minimumLedgerAmount:UInt128Text}
   495	CallPermission = {callee:TextValue, selector:TextValue}
   496	
   497	ClaimEvidenceRef = {claimId:TextValue,
   498	                    kind:"ContractProperty"|"IntentRefinement"|
   499	                         "TransitionValidity"|"PredecessorHistory",
   500	                    proofDigest:Digest, publicInputDigest:Digest}
   501	ExactPlanStatement = {action:ActionCall, exactEffects:[ExactEffect], exactWrites:[ExactWrite],
   502	                      beforeStateHash:Digest, domain:ExecutionDomain, genesisHash:Digest,
   503	                      instanceId:TextValue, mode:"ExactPlan", nonce:TextValue, predecessors:[Digest],
   504	                      principal:TextValue, program:ProgramRef, requiredClaimRoot:Digest, requiredClaims:[ClaimRequirement],
   505	                      schemaVersion:"moriarty-exact-plan/1", validity:Validity}
   506	OutcomeStatement = {allowedActions:[Identifier], grossDebitCaps:[DebitCap],
   507	                    beforeStateHash:Digest, domain:ExecutionDomain, genesisHash:Digest,
   508	                    instanceId:TextValue, minimumNetCredits:[NetGoal], mode:"IntentRefinement",
   509	                    nonce:TextValue, permittedCalls:[CallPermission],
   510	                    permittedRecipients:[TextValue], predecessors:[Digest],
   511	                    principal:TextValue, program:ProgramRef, requiredClaimRoot:Digest, requiredClaims:[ClaimRequirement],
   512	                    schemaVersion:"moriarty-outcome-intent/1", validity:Validity}
   513	ExactPlanAuthority = {domain:"MORIARTY-SIGN-bounded-atomic/1",
   514	                      schemaVersion:"moriarty-authority/1", signature:Signature,
   515	                      statement:ExactPlanStatement, tag:"ExactPlan"}
   516	IntentRefinementAuthority = {domain:"MORIARTY-OUTCOME-bounded-atomic/1",
   517	                             schemaVersion:"moriarty-authority/1", signature:Signature,
   518	                             statement:OutcomeStatement, tag:"IntentRefinement"}
   519	Authority = ExactPlanAuthority | IntentRefinementAuthority
   520	```
   521	
   522	Signature bytes attest exactly
   523	`UTF8(authority.domain) || 00 || canonical(authority.statement)`. The statement's
   524	compact `ProgramRef`, principal, nonce, interval, predecessors, and claim requirements
   525	are therefore signed. ExactPlan compares canonical `exactWrites` against the projection of produced
   526	writes to `{field,value}`, in statement order, and compares each `exactEffects[i].effect` with the complete enriched
   527	`effects[i]` in emit order, with identical array lengths. IntentRefinement requires the selected action be allowed,
   528	all Transfer/Fee recipients and any calls be permitted, every outgoing principal Transfer/Fee have a matching cap and gross principal
   529	debits not exceed it without netting refunds. Caps and goals may name only the
   530	principal-bound actor. Due effects do not enter ledger debit/credit sums.
   531	Each net goal requires checked credits >= checked(debits + minimumLedgerAmount),
   532	with overflow rejecting, in ledger units after exact quantum conversion. This language has no call instruction, so
   533	`permittedCalls` must be empty. Array members use manifest/effect order; caps and
   534	goals sort by `(actor UTF-8, asset UTF-8)` and have no duplicate pair.
   535	
   536	```text
   537	ExternalChecks = {authenticatedPrincipal:TextValue, genesisValid:boolean,
   538	                  nonceFresh:boolean, observationsAuthentic:boolean,
   539	                  predecessorSetValid:boolean, signatureValid:boolean,
   540	                  stateCurrentAndUnconsumed:boolean}
   541	EvaluationInput = {action:ActionCall, authority:Authority, checks:ExternalChecks,
   542	                   genesis:Genesis, observations:ObservationSet,
   543	                   program:ProgramRef, schemaVersion:"moriarty-evaluation/1",
   544	                   state:StateEnvelope}
   545	```
   546	
   547	`authenticatedPrincipal` must equal the signed principal; exactly one genesis
   548	binding for it must exist; its actor must equal the action's mandatory `actor`
   549	argument. Every boolean precheck must be true. The trusted wrapper constructs
   550	these checks for the exact program, genesis, state, action, signed Authority and
   551	ObservationSet supplied in this EvaluationInput. It authenticates these concrete
   552	objects before derivation; there is no dependency on a future trace or proof.
   553	The checks cannot be copied from an unauthenticated client or reused with changed
   554	objects. EvaluationInput carries neither claim evidence nor proof verdicts.
   555	Missing cryptographic/oracle/current-state precheck implementations fail closed.
   556	Post-trace proof verification and final durable consumption are separate below.
   557	
   558	## Settlement and result records
   559	
   560	```text
   561	SettlementResolution = {asset:TextValue, binding:Identifier,
   562	                        ledgerAmount:UInt128Text, nominalAmount:AmountValue,
   563	                        quantum:AmountValue, unit:Identifier}
   564	TransferEffect = {amount:AmountValue, asset:TextValue, from:TextValue,
   565	                  kind:"Transfer", ordinal:UInt128Text,
   566	                  settlement:SettlementResolution, to:TextValue}
   567	FeeEffect      = {amount:AmountValue, asset:TextValue, from:TextValue,
   568	                  kind:"Fee", ordinal:UInt128Text,
   569	                  settlement:SettlementResolution, to:TextValue}
   570	DueCreatedEffect = {amount:AmountValue, creditor:TextValue, debtor:TextValue,
   571	                    denomination:TextValue, dueId:TextValue,
   572	                    kind:"DueCreated", ordinal:UInt128Text}
   573	DueSettledEffect = {amount:AmountValue, asset:TextValue, creditor:TextValue,
   574	                    debtor:TextValue, denomination:TextValue, dueId:TextValue,
   575	                    kind:"DueSettled", ordinal:UInt128Text,
   576	                    settlement:SettlementResolution}
   577	EffectRecord = TransferEffect | FeeEffect | DueCreatedEffect | DueSettledEffect
   578	WriteRecord = {field:Identifier, policy:PolicyUse, value:StoredValue}
   579	ObligationDelta = {created:[ObligationRecord], settled:[ObligationRecord]}
   580	AuthorityConsumption = {mode:"ExactPlan"|"IntentRefinement", nonce:TextValue,
   581	                        principal:TextValue, statementDigest:Digest}
   582	ResourceCounts = {canonicalDepth:UInt128Text, canonicalNodes:UInt128Text,
   583	                  canonicalUtf8Bytes:UInt128Text, effects:UInt128Text,
   584	                  executedInstructions:UInt128Text, expressionNodes:UInt128Text,
   585	                  maximumExpressionDepth:UInt128Text, unitComponents:UInt128Text}
   586	```
   587	
   588	The `ordinal` on every result effect is its zero-based ordinal among all emits in
   589	the selected action. A settlement resolution is required for Transfer, Fee, and
   590	DueSettled and repeats the exact checked binding; it is not an optional side map.
   591	
   592	```text
   593	DiagnosticCode = one exact code from semantics.md "Closed diagnostics"
   594	Diagnostic = {code:DiagnosticCode, message:TextValue, primarySpan:Span,
   595	              relatedSpans:[Span], stage:UInt128Text}
   596	Rejected = {diagnostics:[Diagnostic], outcome:"Rejected",
   597	            profile:"moriarty-bounded-atomic/1", programHash:Digest,
   598	            schemaVersion:"moriarty-result/1"}
   599	CompleteBody = {actionHash:Digest, after:StateEnvelope, authorityConsumption:AuthorityConsumption,
   600	                beforeStateHash:Digest, effects:[EffectRecord],
   601	                obligationDelta:ObligationDelta, observationsHash:Digest,
   602	                outcome:"Complete", predecessors:[Digest],
   603	                profile:"moriarty-bounded-atomic/1", programHash:Digest,
   604	                resourceCounts:ResourceCounts, schemaVersion:"moriarty-complete-body/1",
   605	                writes:[WriteRecord]}
   606	Complete = {body:CompleteBody, schemaVersion:"moriarty-result/1", traceHash:Digest}
   607	Result = Rejected | Complete
   608	```
   609	
   610	Rejected has no next state, writes, effects, or trace. `Pending` is deliberately
   611	not a Result variant; a request requiring it produces Rejected with
   612	`UNSUPPORTED_PENDING`. Complete reports statuses and remaining notional inside
   613	`after.body` and retains every bounded obligation record, including settled
   614	identity tombstones. Canonical result limits apply to the whole wrapper and may
   615	reject a transition even if its individual count limits fit.
   616	
   617	
   618	## Binding and acyclic proof acceptance
   619	
   620	Evaluation has a mandatory local ProgramBinding supplied by the deployment, not
   621	by an untrusted EvaluationInput. This implementation-context value carries the
   622	original bounded source bytes and the exact registered bounds bytes, in local fields
   623	`source` and `bounds` respectively. Both fields are UTF-8 byte sequences; source
   624	obeys sourceEncoding and bounds must be the exact registered byte sequence. It is not
   625	a public wire record and is never embedded in signed envelopes. Derivation
   626	re-parses, checks and lowers those bytes, recomputes sourceHash and boundsHash,
   627	and compares the complete supplied BoundProgram to that deterministic result
   628	before stage 9. Missing binding bytes or a mismatch rejects PROGRAM_ENCODING
   629	at stage 8 (a source-map mismatch uses SOURCE_MAP). Implementations may provide
   630	this binding through a constructor-bound context or an explicit extra parameter;
   631	`derive(boundProgram,input,programBinding)` names the explicit form. The
   632	acceptance backend supplies the same local binding before execution. Merely
   633	checking a supplied programHash without source correspondence is insufficient.
   634	
   635	The stage-9 binding equalities in semantics.md are mandatory before execution.
   636	This single-input atomic profile requires predecessors exactly `[beforeStateHash]`,
   637	including the checked genesis state at revision zero. Both signed requiredClaims
   638	and requiredClaimRoot equal the manifest requirements and recomputed claimRoot.
   639	The unsigned candidate CompleteBody.actionHash is recomputed from the exact
   640	EvaluationInput.action for both authority modes; it is never supplied by the
   641	caller as a substitute. This binds even argument changes that leave all effects
   642	and resource counts unchanged.
   643	
   644	```text
   645	ProofContext = {actionHash:Digest, authorityDigest:Digest, beforeStateHash:Digest,
   646	                domain:ExecutionDomain, genesisHash:Digest,
   647	                predecessors:[Digest], program:ProgramRef,
   648	                requiredClaimRoot:Digest, schemaVersion:"moriarty-proof-context/1",
   649	                traceHash:Digest}
   650	ResolvedProofRef = {byteLength:UInt128Text, claimId:TextValue,
   651	                    kind:"ContractProperty"|"IntentRefinement"|
   652	                         "TransitionValidity"|"PredecessorHistory",
   653	                    reference:TextValue}
   654	ProofAcceptanceInput = {claimEvidence:[ClaimEvidenceRef], proofContext:ProofContext,
   655	                        proofs:[ResolvedProofRef],
   656	                        schemaVersion:"moriarty-proof-acceptance-input/1"}
   657	ProofAcceptanceVerdict = {historyProofValid:boolean, proofContextHash:Digest,
   658	                          requiredClaimsValid:boolean,
   659	                          schemaVersion:"moriarty-proof-acceptance-verdict/1"}
   660	```
   661	
   662	Each ProofContext field equals its locally derived value: actionHash equals both
   663	recomputed input actionHash and CompleteBody.actionHash; authorityDigest hashes
   664	the complete signed Authority; program equals the recomputed ProgramRef; traceHash
   665	hashes the candidate CompleteBody; all remaining fields equal the input genesis,
   666	authority, manifest and before-state bindings. ProofAcceptanceInput.proofContext
   667	must equal that entire locally reconstructed context in canonical bytes.
   668	
   669	External resolver contract: a trusted, profile-bound backend receives
   670	ProofAcceptanceInput and a resolver operation `resolve(reference) -> raw bytes | unavailable`.
   671	References are opaque, nonempty TextValue handles within that invocation, never
   672	URLs to fetch implicitly. No proof bytes enter EvaluationInput or signed objects.
   673	`proofs` and `claimEvidence` each contain exactly one entry per manifest requirement,
   674	in requiredClaims order, matching each (kind,claimId), with no omissions or extras.
   675	Each resolved length must equal byteLength. The limits in
   676	bounds.json.externalProofResolution bound count, individual raw bytes and total
   677	raw bytes before verification. The backend resolves each handle once and retains
   678	those immutable bytes for hashing and verification, rejecting unavailable,
   679	changed, oversized or length-mismatched data with PROOF_RESOLUTION.
   680	
   681	Each ClaimEvidenceRef.publicInputDigest equals the recomputed proofContextHash;
   682	proofDigest is SHA256 of the corresponding resolved raw bytes. The backend verifies
   683	those bytes against the exact required claim kind/ID and complete ProofContext.
   684	Unknown policy claims fail closed. The trusted verdict is accepted only from that
   685	backend invocation, with its proofContextHash equal to the locally recomputed
   686	hash and both booleans true. requiredClaimsValid covers all requirements including
   687	history, and historyProofValid separately affirms every PredecessorHistory claim.
   688	Caller booleans, a matching digest alone, and unavailable backend implementations
   689	cannot stand in for proof verification.
   690	
   691	The numbered protocol is normative:
   692	
   693	1. Compile and bind BoundProgram/genesis, choose the exact ActionCall and before
   694	   state, and obtain authenticated observations. An unsigned simulation may
   695	   calculate proposed writes/effects to construct an ExactPlanStatement; an
   696	   OutcomeStatement instead declares allowed outcomes. This simulation consumes
   697	   nothing and produces no accepted Result.
   698	2. Sign the statement bytes using its registered domain, obtaining Authority.
   699	   The wrapper authenticates that Authority and the exact input tuple and builds
   700	   EvaluationInput containing only the prechecks above.
   701	3. `derive(boundProgram:BoundProgram, input:EvaluationInput, programBinding:ProgramBinding)` validates stages
   702	   9–12 and computes the provisional CompleteBody, stateHash, actionHash,
   703	   traceHash and ProofContext at stage 13. It returns Rejected or an internal
   704	   pair `(candidate:Complete, context:ProofContext)`. This pair is not an accepted
   705	   Result and is not a separate public wire envelope.
   706	4. The prover receives the exact ProofContext, requiredClaims and relation
   707	   witnesses, constructs each mandatory proof, and supplies the bounded resolver
   708	   handles and ClaimEvidenceRef values in ProofAcceptanceInput. Signature and
   709	   trace preimages contain no proof bytes, proofDigest, or ClaimEvidenceRef.
   710	5. `verifyProofs(input:ProofAcceptanceInput, resolver)` performs the resolution,
   711	   digest, claim and context checks above and returns the trusted closed
   712	   ProofAcceptanceVerdict, or a rejection diagnostic at stage 13.
   713	6. `commit(boundProgram, input, candidate, proofAcceptanceInput, verdict)` checks
   714	   equality to the locally derived candidate/context, a successful trusted
   715	   verdict, and the current durable state/nonce again. Only then may the wrapper
   716	   atomically consume state and nonce and expose Complete. A changed consumed
   717	   state or stale nonce rejects; no candidate state or effect is committed.
   718	
   719	The implementing acceptance API is
   720	`evaluate(boundProgram:BoundProgram, input:EvaluationInput, proofAcceptanceBackend)
   721	 -> Rejected | Complete`, where the mandatory backend callback receives the
   722	locally derived ProofContext and requiredClaims, obtains ProofAcceptanceInput
   723	through step 4, and performs step 5. It cannot bypass step 6. This callback
   724	interface makes the two phases explicit without requiring evidence before trace
   725	derivation. MC05 must implement the backend; this source profile supplies no
   726	proof construction or actual verifier. Simulation output is explicitly labeled
   727	simulation and outside accepted Result. The entire sequence remains acyclic.
   728	
   729	## Counts, ordering, and malformed inputs
   730	
   731	ResourceCounts.canonicalDepth, canonicalNodes and canonicalUtf8Bytes measure
   732	canonical CompleteBody with the entire resourceCounts member omitted. This
   733	preimage is called CountBody. Root depth is zero. Each object, array, scalar value
   734	counts as one node; object key strings do not add nodes. Depth increases by one
   735	for each contained value. UTF-8 bytes include all canonical punctuation and keys.
   736	The actual complete wrapper is independently checked against resultEncoding;
   737	CountBody counts never waive that bound. This avoids self-referential byte counts.
   738	Other counts record actually evaluated expression nodes, instructions and effects.
   739	maximumExpressionDepth uses root one for a nonempty set of evaluated expression
   740	roots and is "0" when no expression is evaluated. unitComponents is the maximum
   741	cardinality (number of nonzero UnitComponent records) of an evaluated unit vector;
   742	it is "0" when that set is empty, and also for an action evaluating only
   743	Bool/Text/UInt128 vectors. Static ActionResourceCounts.expressionDepth is likewise
   744	"0" for no expression nodes, otherwise the maximum root-one expression depth.
   745	An empty action has exact static counts
   746	`{effects:"0",expressionDepth:"0",expressionNodes:"0",instructions:"0",locals:"0"}`.
   747	Its dynamic executedInstructions, expressionNodes, effects,
   748	maximumExpressionDepth and unitComponents are all "0". Its canonical byte/node/
   749	depth counts still measure the actual nonempty CountBody, not zero.
   750	 Static ActionResourceCounts include all syntax, even short-circuited
   751	branches. A TypedProgram uses typedProgramEncoding jointly with its embedded AST
   752	bounds. SourceAST.declarations has an effective maximum of 128 total entries,
   753	even when all per-category maxima fit. TypedProgram.annotations contains exactly
   754	one annotation per source expression.
   755	
   756	Named values, arguments, writes and emitted fields follow their declaration or
   757	statement order as applicable. Obligations retain creation order including settled
   758	tombstones. ObligationDelta contains immutable event-time snapshots, each in
   759	its respective emit order: created appends the inserted Outstanding record when
   760	DueCreated succeeds; settled appends the post-update Settled record when
   761	DueSettled succeeds. A later effect must not mutate an earlier delta entry.
   762	If the same action creates and settles one due, both arrays retain that due:
   763	created has status Outstanding, settled has status Settled, and after.obligations
   764	has the Settled record. All other fields in these three records are identical.
   765	Principal
   766	bindings sort by principal UTF-8 bytes, observation bindings/values follow the
   767	manifest observation order, and allowedActions follow manifest action order.
   768	Recipient lists sort by UTF-8 bytes. Duplicate members reject. The signed interval
   769	requires notBefore <= now < notAfterExclusive <= genesis.horizon.
   770	
   771	Parsing/checking returns diagnostics directly before any valid program exists.
   772	Evaluation Rejected uses the verified program hash if available, otherwise the
   773	64-zero digest as an explicit unavailable-program sentinel. A Rejected object
   774	is never a program identity or acceptance certificate. Unknown source spans use
   775	`{startByte:"0",endByte:"0"}`. Diagnostics use stages 1..13 from semantics.md.

     1	# MC01 first language profile proposal
     2	
     3	Status: S2 proposal, not frozen, implemented, or independently approved.
     4	Author: root architect. Date: 2026-09-07 UTC.
     5	Profile identifier: `moriarty-bounded-atomic/1` (proposed).
     6	
     7	This proposal resolves first-profile design choices for MC01 1.1 and D.1/D.2.
     8	It does not close those tasks. The retained complete target crosswalk remains mandatory; fresh Fable and GPT-6
     9	audits of the corrected exact bytes remain required before source-profile freeze.
    10	The controlling acceptance remains the MC01 OpenSpec package at commit `2aa863565fa1faae759f5bef47d2a786534fd627`.
    11	
    12	## Developer surface
    13	
    14	Use a generic bounded transition language for both ACTUS events and DeFi actions.
    15	Financial packages supply checked terms, initial states, event rules, and named properties.
    16	Package names do not create new trusted primitives or prove conformance.
    17	
    18	The proposed source has explicit version, state, action, and effect declarations.
    19	Identifiers use ASCII letters, digits, and underscore, starting with a letter, with at most 64 characters.
    20	Reject reserved prototype names and duplicate declarations in every namespace.
    21	External labels remain typed text values, never object keys or implicit identifiers.
    22	
    23	Illustrative transition fragment, not an executable or complete agreement:
    24	
    25	```text
    26	action accrue_period() {
    27	  guard state.cursor == uint(0), "period already accrued";
    28	  let numerator = state.notional * uint(8) * uint(31);
    29	  let interest = floor_div(numerator, uint(100) * uint(365));
    30	  set principal_due = amount(500000000, USD_micro);
    31	  set interest_due = interest;
    32	  set notional = state.notional - state.principal_due;
    33	  set cursor = uint(1);
    34	  emit DueCreated {
    35	    event: text("lam01:period1:PR"), amount: state.principal_due
    36	  };
    37	}
    38	```
    39	
    40	The complete loan must also emit the interest due and bind each due to its debtor, creditor, denomination, and settlement policy.
    41	The fragment only illustrates syntax and sequential state reads. It is not a valid fixture by itself.
    42	In particular, this proposal does not use a package-selection form as a substitute for an authoring language.
    43	
    44	Proposed statement forms are `guard`, single-definition `let`, `set`, and `emit`.
    45	Proposed expression forms are typed literals, state, argument, observation, constant and local reads, remaining lifetime, comparisons, Boolean operators, multiplication, addition, subtraction, and `floor_div(n,d)`.
    46	There is no slash operator or `floor(e)` expression. Parentheses determine grouping. `not` binds first, then multiplication, then addition/subtraction, comparisons, `and`, and `or`; `floor_div` is a primary call.
    47	Reject chained comparisons. Boolean `and` and `or` short-circuit left to right.
    48	The accompanying grammar.ebnf proposes the EBNF and UTF-8 byte source spans. It requires review before parser implementation.
    49	
    50	There are no user-defined functions, recursion, loops, dynamic evaluation, or unbounded collections in this profile.
    51	Every action is a finite straight-line list. Checked package expansion must satisfy the same final Core bounds.
    52	Unsupported operations produce source-located diagnostics before elaboration.
    53	
    54	## Types, units, and numeric behavior
    55	
    56	Separate `UInt128`, `Bool`, `Text`, nominal `Amount<Unit>`, and intermediate
    57	`Quantity<UnitVector>` in the typed authoring AST and Core. Bool and Quantity are
    58	limited to expressions and lets; neither is persistent state, an input, a set
    59	result, or an effect operand. Every evaluated wire Amount is the closed
    60	`{tag:"Amount",unit,value}` record. Core retains its checked unit vector and source
    61	reference; it does not erase Amount to an untyped integer.
    62	
    63	Use `uint(123)`, `text("123")`, and `amount(123, USD_micro)` as distinct literals.
    64	Numeric text never becomes an integer by inspecting its contents.
    65	Integer tokens are canonical unsigned decimal, with no sign, exponent, separator, or leading zero except `0`.
    66	Reject values above `2^128 - 1`.
    67	
    68	Addition, subtraction, ordering, and equality require equal operand types and equal nominal units.
    69	Multiplication adds unit exponents. Division subtracts them. UInt128 has the empty unit vector.
    70	Compound quantities are expression-only, with at most eight unit components and each exponent between -16 and 16.
    71	Assignment requires the resulting vector to match the declared scalar or nominal amount type exactly.
    72	This permits the swap numerator A*B divided by denominator A to produce B without unchecked unit erasure.
    73	Ordered comparisons accept numeric operands only. Equality also accepts equal Text or Bool types.
    74	Rational rates use explicit numerator and denominator fields. Unit inference checks their use.
    75	Division by zero, underflow, or any intermediate overflow rejects the entire action.
    76	Every intermediate uses the same 128-bit limit, including products before division.
    77	No algebraic rearrangement may change overflow, rounding, or short-circuit behavior.
    78	
    79	Nonnegative integer division rounds down at each explicit division node.
    80	Loan interest uses one final division after the checked products.
    81	Swap pricing uses one final division after the checked numerator and denominator calculations.
    82	There is no hidden floating-point conversion or global financial tolerance.
    83	Every Amount-valued set occurrence and Amount-valued effect-field occurrence has
    84	one explicit policy target. The policy records its unit and the exact action/local
    85	FloorDiv node or `none`. Same-action value provenance deterministically propagates
    86	that node through lets, writes, reads, and arithmetic. Documentary derivation,
    87	remainder, comparison, and proof strings are program-hash-bound but are not parsed
    88	as executable safety predicates.
    89	
    90	`USD_micro` means a millionth of the reference USD denomination.
    91	It does not identify a ledger token. Settlement requires a unique one-to-one unit/
    92	asset binding. A positive quantum `q` means `q` nominal subunits per ledger base
    93	unit; nominal value `v` must divide exactly and produces ledger amount `v/q` with
    94	checked UInt128 arithmetic. A nonzero remainder rejects rather than rounds.
    95	Pool asset units remain distinct, even if both tokens use the same display symbol.
    96	Missing settlement mappings reject settlement construction. Local arithmetic can still display a due.
    97	
    98	Signed financial values require a future version with a defined range, direction, and rounding for negative values.
    99	Calendar operations require a future version with explicit calculation dates, payment dates, and conventions.
   100	Quantity-times-price and bounded convergence require explicit typing and resource extensions.
   101	None may reinterpret existing unsigned bytes or silently fall back to host arithmetic.
   102	
   103	## Canonical representations and finite bounds
   104	
   105	Use an explicit schema version and semantic profile on every public wire envelope.
   106	Canonical keys use the same restricted ASCII identifier alphabet as source identifiers.
   107	External identifiers and event labels are values with separate validation rules.
   108	Use lexicographic ASCII key order, UTF-8 encoding, and JSON string escaping with a single canonical spelling.
   109	Reject duplicate keys before object construction, noncanonical wire bytes, lone surrogates, unknown fields, and numeric JSON tokens.
   110	Do not normalize text silently. Integers on the wire are canonical decimal strings.
   111	Booleans are JSON booleans only where the schema permits them.
   112	
   113	The normative limits are in `bounds.json`. Source, decoded AST, full program manifest,
   114	compact signing envelope, and result are distinct encodings and all applicable limits
   115	hold simultaneously:
   116	
   117	| Resource | Bound |
   118	|---|---:|
   119	| Source, signing envelope, evaluation, or result | 65,536 UTF-8 bytes each |
   120	| Source AST / full manifest / typed program | 262,144 / 524,288 / 1,048,576 UTF-8 bytes |
   121	| AST and full manifest decoded depth, root at zero | 40 |
   122	| Compact signing and result decoded depth, root at zero | 16 |
   123	| Public decoded object nodes | 8,192 |
   124	| AST and manifest / typed program nodes | 32,768 / 65,536 |
   125	| Keys per record | 64 |
   126	| Language Text | 256 UTF-8 bytes |
   127	| Canonical-object text | 4,096 UTF-8 bytes and 4,096 JavaScript code units |
   128	| Each identifier | 64 ASCII characters |
   129	| Entrypoints, state fields, or action fields | 64 each |
   130	| Instructions or locals per entrypoint | 64 each |
   131	| Expression depth, root at one | 16 |
   132	| Total expression nodes per entrypoint | 256 |
   133	| Effects per entrypoint | 16 |
   134	
   135	The SourceAST declaration array has an effective total cap of 128, including reserves.
   136	These are simultaneous limits. A value satisfying one bound may still fail another.
   137	The full program-hash preimage contains Core instructions and uses the manifest depth-40
   138	limit. The depth-16 signing object contains only a compact program reference plus input
   139	and authority data; it never embeds expressions. Both use the exact
   140	`moriarty-canonical-json/1` rules in `typed-schemas.md`. A program is rejected if its
   141	actual aggregate bytes or nodes fail even when every per-action count fits.
   142	Test UTF-8 byte counts independently from JavaScript string lengths.
   143	
   144	Bind the profile's total lifecycle allowance, horizon, and initial state at authenticated genesis.
   145	Every accepted action consumes one allowance, even when it emits no transfer.
   146	No resume, redeployment, split, or join may reset an existing instance's allowance.
   147	The first profile excludes split/join and Pending execution with explicit diagnostics.
   148	Later composition must define conservation of residual budgets before it becomes supported.
   149	
   150	## Transition judgment and correctness scope
   151	
   152	The proposed interface remains:
   153	
   154	```text
   155	parse(source) -> SourceAST | Diagnostic[]
   156	check(ast:SourceAST, profile) -> TypedProgram | Diagnostic[]
   157	elaborate(typed:TypedProgram) -> BoundProgram | Diagnostic[]
   158	derive(boundProgram:BoundProgram, input:EvaluationInput, programBinding:ProgramBinding)
   159	  -> Rejected | (candidate:Complete, context:ProofContext)
   160	verifyProofs(input:ProofAcceptanceInput, resolver)
   161	  -> ProofAcceptanceVerdict | Diagnostic[]
   162	evaluate(boundProgram:BoundProgram, input:EvaluationInput, proofAcceptanceBackend)
   163	  -> Rejected | Complete
   164	```
   165	
   166	EvaluationInput includes genesis, exact action/state/authority/observations,
   167	ProgramRef and trusted prechecks only. The candidate/context pair is an internal
   168	uncommitted derivation, outside accepted Result. The mandatory backend callback
   169	receives that exact ProofContext and requiredClaims, obtains bounded external proof
   170	references in ProofAcceptanceInput, and returns the context-bound trusted verdict.
   171	The numbered sign/derive/prove/verify/commit protocol in typed-schemas.md controls
   172	acceptance; neither evidence nor post-trace verdicts are required before derivation.
   173	Both CompleteBody and ProofContext bind the recomputed hash of input.action.
   174	
   175	`Rejected` returns diagnostics and exposes no committed partial state or effects.
   176	`Complete` means the selected atomic plan completed under its declared outcome policy.
   177	It does not mean the entire agreement or all future financial obligations are discharged.
   178	The result must carry separate agreement status and all residual obligations.
   179	`Pending` is not a result variant in this profile. Requests requiring it reject
   180	with `UNSUPPORTED_PENDING`; a future version must define residual conservation.
   181	
   182	Guards and expressions read the current working state in statement order.
   183	Effects capture values at the emit position. Locals cannot reference future locals.
   184	After successful evaluation, increment revision and decrement remaining allowance exactly once.
   185	The transition receipt includes all writes, effects, obligations, resource counts, and authority consumption.
   186	Expected effects include fees and every permitted intermediate recipient.
   187	Gross debit caps count fees and do not net refunds. Outcome credits are net of fees.
   188	
   189	Keep exact-plan authority and outcome-intent authority as distinct tagged modes.
   190	Preserve historical `IntentEffects` signatures in their original version domain.
   191	Use `IntentRefinement` only with the new explicit mode and semantic domain.
   192	Bind program/profile version, instance, principal, nonce, validity interval, predecessors, and all required claims.
   193	Do not silently relabel old signed statements.
   194	
   195	Local evaluation does not establish durable replay protection, oracle truth, or actual proof acceptance.
   196	MC04/MC05 must enforce these checks at the ledger acceptance boundary.
   197	Every accepted proof must discharge its precise contract, intent, transition, and history predicates.
   198	This proposal supplies no native proof, universal correctness theorem, or production acceptance path.
   199	
   200	## Required developer examples and rejection controls
   201	
   202	The loan example is the first LAM period, not a complete LAM contract.
   203	Initial notional is 5,000,000,000 micro-USD. Principal payment is 500,000,000.
   204	Interest is `floor_div(5,000,000,000 * 8 * 31, 100 * 365) = 33,972,602`.
   205	Settlement is 533,972,602. Remaining contractual notional is 4,500,000,000 after the sample closes.
   206	Expose that remainder explicitly. Never display the sample's `closed` flag as debt-free status.
   207	Retain both PR and IP obligation identities despite their combined sample transition.
   208	The floor-rounded sample is not exact conformance to the upstream finite-decimal cashflow.
   209	
   210	The swap example uses reserves 1,000,000 and 2,000,000, input 10,000, and price multiplier 997/1000.
   211	Expected output is 19,743. New reserves are 1,010,000 and 1,980,257.
   212	Pricing retains the fee in the input reserve. It does not emit an extra fee transfer.
   213	The explicit `reserve swap for close;` declaration requires the exact
   214	`remaining > uint(1)` guard on swap. It preserves one allowance for close, but
   215	proofs, guards, resources and result size can still prevent closure. A bounded
   216	state can have no representable Complete wrapper; this accepted profile limitation
   217	rejects RESULT_BOUNDS without consumption and carries no blanket liveness claim.
   218	
   219	Implementation must compare every field against the independent retained reference calculations.
   220	Required negative examples include wrong units, denominator zero, overflowing numerator, and numeric-looking text.
   221	Also reject wrong settlement asset/recipient, wrong event order, insufficient minOut, extra effects, and exhausted lifetime.
   222	Include a positive boundary example beside every capacity or authority rejection.
   223	
   224	## Target extension obligations
   225	
   226	| Decision | MC01 foundation | MC07 obligation |
   227	|---|---|---|
   228	| DS-01 | Distinct calculation/payment dates and versioned event order | Exact business-day semantics and pam08/pam09 |
   229	| DS-02 | Lossless convention identifiers | CSMP/SCMP source discrepancy |
   230	| DS-03 | Explicit numeric and bounded-iteration extension boundary | Independently derive and check ANN initialization |
   231	| DS-04 | Defined signed-value and quantity/price extension boundary | COM cashflow signs and quantity rule |
   232	| DS-05 | Finite lifetime, horizon, observations, and closure reserve | CLM call/redemption behavior |
   233	| DS-06 | Stable event IDs with separate external labels | FXOUT settlement vector and labels |
   234	| DS-07 | Separate episode and full-contract scope | FUTUR margin behavior and taxonomy discrepancy |
   235	
   236	The two examples do not establish coverage of the complete target corpus.
   237	The retained complete 32-row ACTUS requirements and 72-row DeFi crosswalk is unchanged and remains required before profile freeze.
   238	Every row must state supported behavior, rejected behavior, required extension, source locator, and evidence status.
   239	All 277 ACTUS fixtures and 72 DeFi rows remain required for MC07 acceptance.

   242	
   243	Preserve checked 128-bit arithmetic and exact evaluation order during lowering.
   244	Insert range, underflow, and divisor checks where the target does not enforce the source rule directly.
   245	Maintain source maps for guards, assignments, and effects.
   246	Reject unsupported nominal-unit erasure, wire encodings, dynamic effects, and unbound settlement policies.
   247	Require generated positive loan/swap examples and mutation tests before calling the mapping implemented.
   248	Successful compilation alone is not compiler-to-ledger correspondence.

   253	Recompute program identifiers, signature domains, proofs, certificates, theorem instances, and audits affected by that change.
   254	Historical artifacts remain scoped to their original bytes and semantics.

   286	## Binding equalities
   287	
   288	Stage 9 uses canonical byte equality for records/arrays and exact scalar equality.
   289	The independently supplied BoundProgram is rechecked and its ProgramRef recomputed.
   290	These predicates are mandatory; the code in parentheses rejects a mismatch:
   291	
   292	- input.program equals that recomputed ProgramRef; manifest.bounds equals
   293	  input.program.bounds and the actual bounds registry reference (`PROGRAM_BINDING`).
   294	- genesis.body.program equals input.program (`GENESIS_PROGRAM_BINDING`).
   295	- genesis.body.initialState equals manifest.initialState, preserving initial
   296	  literals and declaration order (`GENESIS_INITIAL_STATE_BINDING`).
   297	- genesis.body.lifetime equals manifest.lifetime (`GENESIS_LIFETIME_BINDING`).
   298	- genesis.body.horizon equals manifest.horizon (`GENESIS_HORIZON_BINDING`).
   299	- genesis.body.bounds equals input.program.bounds (`GENESIS_BOUNDS_BINDING`).

   301	- state.body.genesisHash equals genesis.genesisHash (`STATE_GENESIS_BINDING`).
   302	- state.body.instanceId equals genesis.body.instanceId (`INSTANCE_BINDING`).
   303	- authority.statement.program equals input.program (`AUTHORITY_PROGRAM_BINDING`);
   304	  its domain, genesisHash, instanceId and beforeStateHash equal genesis.body.domain,
   305	  genesis.genesisHash, genesis.body.instanceId and state.stateHash respectively
   306	  (`AUTHORITY_CONTEXT_BINDING`).
   307	- authority.statement.requiredClaims equals manifest.requiredClaims, and both its
   308	  requiredClaimRoot and genesis.body.requiredClaimRoot equal the recomputed
   309	  manifest claimRoot (`CLAIM_ROOT_BINDING`).
   310	- authority.statement.predecessors equals exactly `[state.stateHash]`
   311	  (`PREDECESSOR_BINDING`).
   312	- In ExactPlan mode, authority.statement.action equals input.action
   313	  (`EXACT_ACTION_BINDING`). In IntentRefinement mode, input.action.name occurs in
   314	  allowedActions (`INTENT_ACTION_FORBIDDEN`); every allowed name is declared.
   315	
   316	All supplied digest wrappers are independently recomputed (`DIGEST_MISMATCH`).
   317	Action name, argument names/order/types, state values/schema and observation
   318	names/order/types must match the manifest (`INPUT_SCHEMA`). State obligations
   319	have unique due IDs, positive correctly denominated amounts and declared units
   320	(`INPUT_SCHEMA`). At revision zero, state values equal manifest.initialState,
   321	remaining equals lifetime, obligations are empty and statuses are derived exactly
   322	(`GENESIS_STATE_BINDING`); later states require authenticated currentness/history.
   323	Stage 10 also checks checked revision + remaining == genesis lifetime, the
   324	exclusive horizon, valid interval, all prechecks and the principal-to-actor rule.
   325	
   326	## Authority, observations, and external assumptions
   327	
   328	Evaluation receives the exact closed `EvaluationInput` in `typed-schemas.md`.
   329	The compact ProgramRef must equal the recomputed full program reference and state/
   330	genesis bindings. The authenticated principal must equal the signed principal;
   331	exactly one genesis PrincipalBinding must map it to an actor; and that actor must
   332	equal the action's required `actor` Text argument. Thus source guards such as
   333	`arg.actor == const.borrower` constrain a program role, while the mandatory
   334	wrapper binding proves which authenticated principal supplied that actor. Text
   335	equality alone is never authentication.
   336	
   337	Every declared observation appears exactly once, with the genesis-bound provider
   338	and correct stored type. `now` is mandatory. The wrapper authenticates observation
   339	evidence and oracle/provider identity. The language neither asserts oracle truth
   340	nor permits an undeclared host read.
   341	
   342	ExactPlan authority signs the exact instance, ActionCall, writes, and enriched
   343	effects. IntentRefinement signs the exact instance, allowed actions, recipient/call permissions,
   344	gross debit caps including Fee, minimum net credits after debits and fees,
   345	predecessors, claim requirements, validity, principal, nonce, domain, genesis
   346	hash, input-state hash, and ProgramRef. Proof evidence is not signed; its exact
   347	ProofContext and the acyclic acceptance sequence are in typed-schemas.md. Checks use
   348	ledger amounts after exact quantum conversion. Refunds do not reduce gross debit.
   349	Only the principal-bound actor A may appear in any cap or goal; other actors
   350	reject `INTENT_ACTOR_SCOPE`. Every Transfer or Fee with from == A requires a cap
   351	for (A,asset), even for zero amount, or rejects `INTENT_DEBIT_UNCAPPED`. Checked
   352	sums of those ledger amounts must not exceed the corresponding caps
   353	(`INTENT_DEBIT_CAP`). Refunds never reduce these gross sums. For each goal,
   354	credits sum Transfer/Fee with to == A and debits sum Transfer/Fee with from == A
   355	for that asset. A self-transfer contributes to both sums. DueCreated and
   356	DueSettled contribute to neither sum, avoiding double-counting settlement records.
   357	A missing debit or credit sum is zero. The goal passes exactly when
   358	`checked(credits) >= checked(debits + minimumLedgerAmount)`; failure rejects
   359	`INTENT_NET_GOAL`. Every sum and addition is UInt128 in ledger units after quantum
   360	conversion; overflow rejects `INTENT_ARITHMETIC_OVERFLOW`. All Transfer/Fee
   361	recipients, including nonprincipal transfers, must appear in permittedRecipients
   362	(`INTENT_RECIPIENT_FORBIDDEN`). This language has no external-call effect, so a
   363	nonempty permittedCalls list rejects `INTENT_CALL_UNSUPPORTED`.
   364	
   365	Signature verification, key control, nonce freshness across durable history,
   366	state unconsumed/currentness, predecessor ordering/finality, observation truth,
   367	required contract/intent/transition/history PCD claims bound to the derived
   368	ProofContext, asset authenticity,
   369	balance conservation, and ledger application are external mandatory predicates.
   370	All pre-execution checks and post-trace backend verdicts must be true in their
   371	respective phases; missing implementations fail closed. A hash-linked receipt, local simulation, documentary proof string, or
   372	MockProver result is not PCD. Historical `MORIARTY-SIGN-v1` and
   373	`MORIARTY-OUTCOME-SIGN-v1` bytes/domains remain unchanged and cannot be relabeled
   374	as the new authority variants.
   375	
   376	## Deterministic validation and transition
   377	
   378	Validation occurs in these stages; diagnostics within one stage sort by
   379	`(primarySpan.startByte,primarySpan.endByte,code)`:
   380	
   381	1. UTF-8/source byte validation.
   382	2. Longest-match lexing, exact keyword classification, identifiers, integer and
   383	   JSON-string tokens; comments and plain slash reject.
   384	3. Complete parse; recovery never creates executable AST.
   385	4. Closed declarations, exact effect schemas, uniqueness, counts and AST bounds.
   386	5. Declaration-before-use value/type resolution and deterministic node IDs/spans.
   387	   Policy metadata uses complete-action-table resolution in stage 6.
   388	6. Types, unit vectors, let locality, UInt bounds, policy target/node coverage,
   389	   settlement one-to-one bindings, status rules, actor parameter and explicit
   390	   ReserveRule validation.
   391	7. All simultaneous source, AST, typed-program and program-shape bounds.
   392	   Per-category maxima never waive aggregate limits.
   393	8. Deterministic lowering, source maps, canonical SemanticManifest and full
   394	   BoundProgram representability, bounds hash and program hash recomputation.
   395	9. Closed canonical evaluation/genesis/state/action/authority/observation schemas,
   396	   all digest recomputations, and every listed Binding equality.
   397	10. Current genesis/state/revision/remaining/horizon plus external authority,
   398	    principal/actor, nonce, validity, observation, and predecessor checks.
   399	    EvaluationInput carries prechecks only; no claim evidence exists in this phase.
   400	11. Sequential guard/let/set/emit execution with checked arithmetic and policy
   401	    provenance in a private working copy.
   402	12. Resolve settlement, process obligations and transfer conservation, derive
   403	    candidate statuses/values/revision+1/remaining-1, and check exact-plan or
   404	    outcome refinement, gross/net, resources and full result representability.
   405	13. Finalize candidate stateHash, recomputed actionHash, Complete traceHash and
   406	    ProofContext without commit. Receive the separate ProofAcceptanceInput,
   407	    resolve bounded proof bytes and verify all mandatory claims against that exact
   408	    context. Accept only the matching trusted ProofAcceptanceVerdict and recheck
   409	    durable currentness/nonce atomically with commit. Any failure returns Rejected.
   410	    The numbered two-phase protocol and implementing API are in typed-schemas.md.
   411	
   412	Complete means the selected atomic action and signed/refined outcome completed.
   413	It does not mean the full contract is discharged. Pending is not a result variant;
   414	a request needing residual progress rejects `UNSUPPORTED_PENDING` before commit.
   415	
   416	## Closed diagnostics
   417	
   418	This is the closed DiagnosticCode registry. No other code is legal in this profile.
   419	Each code has exactly the stage below; a condition described elsewhere maps to
   420	this table even where its prose does not repeat the code. Malformed data stops
   421	before dependent semantic checks. Validate stages in numeric order and stop at
   422	the first failing stage; for execution-dependent stages, stop at the first failing
   423	operation in the specified evaluation/effect order. Where independent errors at
   424	that point coexist, choose the smallest `(primarySpan.startByte,primarySpan.endByte,code)`.
   425	Return exactly one Diagnostic (therefore the rejection itself remains bounded).
   426	Use the failing source expression/statement/declaration span when available, or
   427	`{startByte:"0",endByte:"0"}` for input/backend/global errors without a source node.
   428	`relatedSpans` is `[]`; `message` equals the code, except GUARD_FAILED uses the
   429	exact source guard message. Parsing/checking returns this same diagnostic directly.
